\DoxyCodeLine{277         \textcolor{comment}{//GI 03/02/2023: Based on a issue regarding data leak after failed initialisation, I implement}}
\DoxyCodeLine{278         \textcolor{comment}{//this solution to robustly clean the heap allocated variables after a failed object initilisation.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{279         \textcolor{comment}{//Indeed, in such cases, the object is never build, so the desctructor is not available.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{280         \textcolor{comment}{//So, I have implemented a private method default\_destructor that is called from the destructor to clean the heap}}
\DoxyCodeLine{281         \textcolor{comment}{//but can be called also here. So we use a try-\/catch-\/rethrow schema to check if the initilisation}}
\DoxyCodeLine{282         \textcolor{comment}{//was successful, otherwise we clean the heap and then rethrow the error.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{283         \textcolor{comment}{//We use this schema also for the other two constructors.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{284 }
\DoxyCodeLine{285         \textcolor{comment}{//Try the initialisation}}
\DoxyCodeLine{286         \textcolor{keywordflow}{try} \{}
\DoxyCodeLine{287             \mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a0d1d22a199d453e485f2f2d576b284e1}{default\_initialiser}}(\textcolor{keyword}{false}, pureHE);}
\DoxyCodeLine{288             \textcolor{comment}{//TODO, Notice set\_rzams and the Inertia set could be in principle put inside the default initiliaser}}
\DoxyCodeLine{289             \textcolor{comment}{//However in this case, each time a Star is initialised, even if just an auxiliary star, two stars are initialised}}
\DoxyCodeLine{290             \textcolor{comment}{//instead (the second one is the star initialised at time zams to get rzmas). This could represent a significant overhead when}}
\DoxyCodeLine{291             \textcolor{comment}{//a lot of auxiliary stars are created. Therefore, we decided to set rzams only when a true star is initialised (with the current constructor)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{292             \textcolor{comment}{//set rzams and rzams0}}
\DoxyCodeLine{293             \mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_aae7dffd2f27b61c310f3403b43785cdb}{set\_rzams}}(); \textcolor{comment}{//set rzams}}
\DoxyCodeLine{294             \mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a1f458d4c76b01c5f6e5d09a7d3bcd9b2}{rzams0}} = \mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a1497837df53291a47a8988099c0fea59}{rzams}};}
\DoxyCodeLine{295             \textcolor{comment}{//Set propertly the inertia that could need to know rzams}}
\DoxyCodeLine{296             \textcolor{keywordflow}{if} (!\mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a726ad03ed9c2ef7293bd0ca119dd0752}{get\_svpar\_bool}}(\textcolor{stringliteral}{"{}tabuse\_inertia"{}}) and !\mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a5393a0ad9bc22851d12c390c5fb9c0b4}{amiremnant}}()) \mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a665d2054c57f60d9a48ab1e9de9ff6a3}{properties}}[\mbox{\hyperlink{class_inertia_af8a8096a0679ecaf5b629674e180fb5e}{Inertia::ID}}]-\/>evolve(\textcolor{keyword}{this});}
\DoxyCodeLine{297         \}}
\DoxyCodeLine{298         \textcolor{comment}{//Catch any kind of error, dangerous in general, but we use it just to clean the heap and then}}
\DoxyCodeLine{299         \textcolor{comment}{//the error is rethrown and handled somewhere else.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{300         \textcolor{keywordflow}{catch} (...)\{ \textcolor{comment}{//Catch all errors}}
\DoxyCodeLine{301             \mbox{\hyperlink{class_star_a685aa259384cc3bddd15bdb7ba4fc406}{default\_destructor}}();  \textcolor{comment}{//Clean the heap associated to class members}}
\DoxyCodeLine{302             \textcolor{keywordflow}{throw}; \textcolor{comment}{//Rethrown the exact same  error}}
\DoxyCodeLine{303         \}}
\DoxyCodeLine{304     \}}
\DoxyCodeLine{305 }
